### [186] [The Density of Cross-Persistence Diagrams and Its Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11623)
*Alexander Mironenko,Evgeny. Burnaev,Serguei Barannikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了交叉持续图密度，证明了其存在性，建立了统计理论基础，并设计了从点云坐标和距离矩阵直接预测交叉持续密度的首个机器学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续图能分析单个流形的拓扑特征，但无法捕捉两个点云之间的相互作用关系。交叉持续图虽能表征两个点云拓扑特征间的关系，但其密度特性尚未得到系统研究，限制了在统计分析和机器学习中的应用。

Method: 1) 证明交叉持续图密度的存在性并建立理论统计基础；2) 设计首个机器学习框架，直接从点云坐标和距离矩阵预测交叉持续密度；3) 利用交叉持续图的线性特性区分不同流形采样的点云；4) 探索噪声在增强点云区分能力中的新效用。

Result: 在多样化数据集上的实验表明，该方法在密度预测方面持续优于现有技术，在点云区分任务中取得优异结果。有趣的是，引入噪声能增强区分点云的能力，揭示了噪声在TDA应用中的新效用。

Conclusion: 该研究为交叉持续图密度提供了首个系统性分析，建立了理论基础和实用机器学习框架，扩展了交叉持续图在数据分析中的应用范围，包括时间序列领域任务和AI生成文本的几何分析等新方向。

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) provides powerful tools to explore the shape and structure of data through topological features such as clusters, loops, and voids. Persistence diagrams are a cornerstone of TDA, capturing the evolution of these features across scales. While effective for analyzing individual manifolds, persistence diagrams do not account for interactions between pairs of them. Cross-persistence diagrams (cross-barcodes), introduced recently, address this limitation by characterizing relationships between topological features of two point clouds. In this work, we present the first systematic study of the density of cross-persistence diagrams. We prove its existence, establish theoretical foundations for its statistical use, and design the first machine learning framework for predicting cross-persistence density directly from point cloud coordinates and distance matrices. Our statistical approach enables the distinction of point clouds sampled from different manifolds by leveraging the linear characteristics of cross-persistence diagrams. Interestingly, we find that introducing noise can enhance our ability to distinguish point clouds, uncovering its novel utility in TDA applications. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods through experiments on diverse datasets, where our approach consistently outperforms existing techniques in density prediction and achieves superior results in point cloud distinction tasks. Our findings contribute to a broader understanding of cross-persistence diagrams and open new avenues for their application in data analysis, including potential insights into time-series domain tasks and the geometry of AI-generated texts. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Verdangeta/TDA_experiments

</details>


### [187] [VisDoT : Enhancing Visual Reasoning through Human-Like Interpretation Grounding and Decomposition of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11631)
*Eunsoo Lee,Jeongwoo Lee,Minki Hong,Jangho Choi,Jihie Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VisDoT框架通过人类感知式视觉基元检测和思维分解提示，提升大视觉语言模型在图表理解和视觉推理中的性能，在多个基准测试中取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型在检测图表中的视觉基元并将其与语义表示对齐方面存在困难，这严重限制了其在复杂视觉推理中的表现。缺乏感知基础是图表推理的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出VisDoT框架，基于图形感知理论形式化四个感知任务（包括位置和长度）。引入思维分解提示，将问题顺序分解为视觉感知子问题和逻辑子问题。使用VisDoT对InternVL进行微调。

Result: 在ChartQA上提升+11.2%，在更难的ChartQAPro基准上超越GPT-4o。在新引入的VisDoTQA基准上提升+33.2%。在多样开放域VQA基准上获得一致的零样本增益，证实了感知-逻辑分离策略的泛化性。

Conclusion: VisDoT通过人类感知式视觉基础增强了视觉推理，实现了最先进的图表理解和可解释的视觉推理，感知-逻辑分离策略具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) struggle to reliably detect visual primitives in charts and align them with semantic representations, which severely limits their performance on complex visual reasoning. This lack of perceptual grounding constitutes a major bottleneck for chart-based reasoning. We propose VisDoT, a framework that enhances visual reasoning through human-like interpretation grounding. We formalize four perceptual tasks based on the theory of graphical perception, including position and length. Building on this foundation, we introduce Decomposition-of-Thought (DoT) prompting, which sequentially separates questions into visual perception sub-questions and logic sub-questions. Fine-tuning InternVL with VisDoT achieves a +11.2% improvement on ChartQA and surpasses GPT-4o on the more challenging ChartQAPro benchmark. On the newly introduced VisDoTQA benchmark, the model improves by +33.2%. Furthermore, consistent zero-shot gains on diverse open-domain VQA benchmarks confirm the generalizability of the perception-logic separation strategy for visual question answering. VisDoT leverages human-like perception to enhance visual grounding, achieving state-of-the-art chart understanding and interpretable visual reasoning.

</details>


### [188] [LLMs can construct powerful representations and streamline sample-efficient supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11679)
*Ilker Demirel,Larry Shi,Zeshan Hussain,David Sontag*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的rubric方法，将异构医疗数据转换为标准化格式，在15个临床任务上显著优于传统方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据集日益复杂和异构，监督学习受限于输入表示设计。处理多模态数据（如时间序列、自由文本、结构化记录）需要大量领域特定工程

Method: 提出agentic pipeline：1) LLM分析文本序列化输入样本，合成全局rubric作为程序化规范；2) 使用rubric将原始文本序列化输入转换为标准化格式；3) 还提出任务条件化的局部rubric

Result: 在EHRSHOT基准的15个临床任务上，rubric方法显著优于传统计数特征模型、朴素文本序列化LLM基线以及预训练大量数据的临床基础模型

Conclusion: Rubric方法不仅性能优越，在医疗操作环境中还具有易审计、部署成本低、可转换为表格表示等优势，能解锁多种机器学习技术

Abstract: As real-world datasets become increasingly complex and heterogeneous, supervised learning is often bottlenecked by input representation design. Modeling multimodal data for downstream tasks, such as time-series, free text, and structured records, often requires non-trivial domain-specific engineering. We propose an agentic pipeline to streamline this process. First, an LLM analyzes a small but diverse subset of text-serialized input examples in-context to synthesize a global rubric, which acts as a programmatic specification for extracting and organizing evidence. This rubric is then used to transform naive text-serializations of inputs into a more standardized format for downstream models. We also describe local rubrics, which are task-conditioned summaries generated by an LLM. Across 15 clinical tasks from the EHRSHOT benchmark, our rubric-based approaches significantly outperform traditional count-feature models, naive text-serialization-based LLM baselines, and a clinical foundation model, which is pretrained on orders of magnitude more data. Beyond performance, rubrics offer several advantages for operational healthcare settings such as being easy to audit, cost-effectiveness to deploy at scale, and they can be converted to tabular representations that unlock a swath of machine learning techniques.

</details>


### [189] [Explicit Logic Channel for Validation and Enhancement of MLLMs on Zero-Shot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11689)
*Mei Chee Leong,Ying Gu,Hui Li Tan,Liyuan Li,Nancy Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种与黑盒多模态大语言模型并行的显式逻辑通道，用于模型验证、选择和增强，通过一致性率进行跨通道验证，并在零样本任务中提升模型性能和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态大语言模型在视觉语言理解任务中表现出色，但通常以黑盒方式部署为零样本解决方案。验证和理解这些模型的行为对于新任务应用变得重要，需要增强模型的可解释性和可信度。

Method: 提出显式逻辑通道与黑盒模型通道并行工作。显式逻辑通道模仿人类逻辑推理，包含LLM、VFM以及基于概率推理的事实、反事实和关系推理。提出一致性率用于跨通道验证和模型选择，无需真实标注。通过跨通道集成提升零样本任务性能。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的基准测试上，对来自4个前沿家族的11个开源MLLM进行了系统评估，涵盖MC-VQA和HC-REC两个代表性VLC任务。实验证明了ELC和CR在模型验证、选择和改进方面的有效性，同时增强了可解释性和可信度。

Conclusion: 显式逻辑通道和一致性率为多模态大语言模型提供了有效的验证、选择和增强框架，通过显式视觉证据增强了模型的可信度，为黑盒模型的部署和应用提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Frontier Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities in Visual-Language Comprehension (VLC) tasks. However, they are often deployed as zero-shot solution to new tasks in a black-box manner. Validating and understanding the behavior of these models become important for application to new task. We propose an Explicit Logic Channel, in parallel with the black-box model channel, to perform explicit logical reasoning for model validation, selection and enhancement. The frontier MLLM, encapsulating latent vision-language knowledge, can be considered as an Implicit Logic Channel. The proposed Explicit Logic Channel, mimicking human logical reasoning, incorporates a LLM, a VFM, and logical reasoning with probabilistic inference for factual, counterfactual, and relational reasoning over the explicit visual evidence. A Consistency Rate (CR) is proposed for cross-channel validation and model selection, even without ground-truth annotations. Additionally, cross-channel integration further improves performance in zero-shot tasks over MLLMs, grounded with explicit visual evidence to enhance trustworthiness. Comprehensive experiments conducted for two representative VLC tasks, i.e., MC-VQA and HC-REC, on three challenging benchmarks, with 11 recent open-source MLLMs from 4 frontier families. Our systematic evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of proposed ELC and CR for model validation, selection and improvement on MLLMs with enhanced explainability and trustworthiness.

</details>


### [190] [When OpenClaw Meets Hospital: Toward an Agentic Operating System for Dynamic Clinical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11721)
*Wenxian Yang,Hanzheng Qiu,Bangqun Zhang,Chengquan Li,Zhiyong Huang,Xiaobin Feng,Rongshan Yu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向医院环境的LLM智能体架构，通过受限执行环境、文档中心交互、页面索引内存和医学技能库四大组件，构建安全的医院智能体操作系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体在改善临床工作流程方面具有潜力，但由于可靠性限制、安全风险和长期记忆机制不足，在医疗环境中部署自主智能体仍然困难。需要设计一个既保持智能体能力又确保安全性的架构。

Method: 提出包含四个核心组件的架构：1) 受Linux多用户系统启发的受限执行环境；2) 连接患者和临床医生智能体的文档中心交互范式；3) 为长期临床上下文管理设计的页面索引内存架构；4) 支持临床任务序列临时组合的医学技能库。通过预定义技能接口和资源隔离约束智能体行为。

Result: 该架构形成了医院智能体操作系统的基础，能够协调临床工作流程同时保持安全性、透明度和可审计性。基于OpenClaw开源自主智能体框架实现，将其能力结构化为离散技能库，并增加了临床部署所需的基础设施级约束。

Conclusion: 提出的架构为LLM智能体在医疗环境中的安全部署提供了系统化解决方案，通过受限执行、文档中心交互、长期记忆管理和技能库组合，平衡了智能体能力与医疗安全要求，为医院智能体操作系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents extend conventional generative models by integrating reasoning, tool invocation, and persistent memory. Recent studies suggest that such agents may significantly improve clinical workflows by automating documentation, coordinating care processes, and assisting medical decision making. However, despite rapid progress, deploying autonomous agents in healthcare environments remains difficult due to reliability limitations, security risks, and insufficient long-term memory mechanisms. This work proposes an architecture that adapts LLM agents for hospital environments. The design introduces four core components: a restricted execution environment inspired by Linux multi-user systems, a document-centric interaction paradigm connecting patient and clinician agents, a page-indexed memory architecture designed for long-term clinical context management, and a curated medical skills library enabling ad-hoc composition of clinical task sequences. Rather than granting agents unrestricted system access, the architecture constrains actions through predefined skill interfaces and resource isolation. We argue that such a system forms the basis of an Agentic Operating System for Hospital, a computing layer capable of coordinating clinical workflows while maintaining safety, transparency, and auditability. This work grounds the design in OpenClaw, an open-source autonomous agent framework that structures agent capabilities as a curated library of discrete skills, and extends it with the infrastructure-level constraints required for safe clinical deployment.

</details>


### [191] [Anomaly detection in time-series via inductive biases in the latent space of conditional normalizing flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11756)
*David Baumgartner,Eliezer de Souza da Silva,Iñigo Urteaga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于条件归一化流和状态空间框架的多元时间序列异常检测方法，将异常定义从观测空间迁移到潜在空间，通过潜在轨迹的合规性测试进行异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于数据似然最大化的深度生成模型在异常检测中存在结构性限制：观测空间的似然度量的是边际密度而非结构化时序动态的符合性，因此可能给异常或分布外样本分配高概率。

Method: 在条件归一化流中引入显式归纳偏置，在离散时间状态空间框架下建模时间序列观测，约束潜在表示按照规定的时序动态演化。异常检测简化为统计基础的合规性测试，将观测映射到潜在空间并通过拟合优度检验评估其是否符合规定的潜在演化。

Result: 在合成和真实世界时间序列上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地检测频率、幅度和观测噪声方面的异常，同时提供模型合规性的可解释诊断。

Conclusion: 通过将异常概念重新定位到规定的潜在空间，该方法克服了传统基于似然的异常检测的结构性限制，提供了基于统计合规性测试的原则性决策规则，即使在观测似然高的区域也能保持有效性。

Abstract: Deep generative models for anomaly detection in multivariate time-series are typically trained by maximizing data likelihood. However, likelihood in observation space measures marginal density rather than conformity to structured temporal dynamics, and therefore can assign high probability to anomalous or out-of-distribution samples. We address this structural limitation by relocating the notion of anomaly to a prescribed latent space. We introduce explicit inductive biases in conditional normalizing flows, modeling time-series observations within a discrete-time state-space framework that constrains latent representations to evolve according to prescribed temporal dynamics. Under this formulation, expected behavior corresponds to compliance with a specified distribution over latent trajectories, while anomalies are defined as violations of these dynamics. Anomaly detection is consequently reduced to a statistically grounded compliance test, such that observations are mapped to latent space and evaluated via goodness-of-fit tests against the prescribed latent evolution. This yields a principled decision rule that remains effective even in regions of high observation likelihood. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series demonstrate reliable detection of anomalies in frequency, amplitude, and observation noise, while providing interpretable diagnostics of model compliance.

</details>


### [192] [Understanding Wikidata Qualifiers: An Analysis and Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11767)
*Gilles Falquet,Sahar Aljalbout*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对Wikidata限定符进行深入分析，提出基于频率和多样性的分类方法，构建包含上下文、认知/不确定性、结构和附加限定符的分类体系，旨在改善限定符选择、图查询和逻辑推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Wikidata限定符在语义理解、实际使用中的挑战，包括如何选择合适限定符、查询图数据以及进行逻辑推理，为贡献者提供指导并改进限定符推荐系统。

Method: 分析Wikidata数据转储，基于频率和多样性评估限定符重要性，使用改进的香农熵指数处理"长尾"现象，选取前300个限定符进行分类，构建包含上下文、认知/不确定性、结构和附加限定符的细化分类体系。

Result: 成功识别并分类了最重要的限定符，提出的分类体系有效覆盖了关键限定符，为贡献者创建和查询陈述提供了结构化方法，能够指导限定符推荐系统改进和知识图谱设计方法优化。

Conclusion: 该研究开发的限定符分类体系为Wikidata贡献者提供了实用指导，改善了限定符选择、查询和推理过程，对知识图谱设计和限定符推荐系统有重要应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an in-depth analysis of Wikidata qualifiers, focusing on their semantics and actual usage, with the aim of developing a taxonomy that addresses the challenges of selecting appropriate qualifiers, querying the graph, and making logical inferences. The study evaluates qualifier importance based on frequency and diversity, using a modified Shannon entropy index to account for the "long tail" phenomenon. By analyzing a Wikidata dump, the top 300 qualifiers were selected and categorized into a refined taxonomy that includes contextual, epistemic/uncertainty, structural, and additional qualifiers. The taxonomy aims to guide contributors in creating and querying statements, improve qualifier recommendation systems, and enhance knowledge graph design methodologies. The results show that the taxonomy effectively covers the most important qualifiers and provides a structured approach to understanding and utilizing qualifiers in Wikidata.

</details>


### [193] [Governing Evolving Memory in LLM Agents: Risks, Mechanisms, and the Stability and Safety Governed Memory (SSGM) Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11768)
*Chingkwun Lam,Jiaxin Li,Lingfei Zhang,Kuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SSGM框架：针对LLM智能体动态记忆系统的稳定性与安全治理架构，通过一致性验证、时间衰减建模和动态访问控制来缓解记忆腐败风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体记忆系统从静态检索数据库转向动态、自主的机制，出现了记忆治理、语义漂移和隐私漏洞等关键问题。现有研究主要关注记忆检索效率，忽视了高度动态环境中记忆腐败的新兴风险

Method: 提出稳定性与安全治理记忆（SSGM）框架，这是一个概念性治理架构。SSGM通过执行一致性验证、时间衰减建模和动态访问控制，将记忆演化与执行解耦，确保在记忆巩固前进行治理检查

Result: 通过形式化分析和架构分解，证明SSGM能够缓解拓扑诱导的知识泄漏（敏感上下文固化为长期存储）和防止语义漂移（知识通过迭代摘要而退化）

Conclusion: 该工作提供了记忆腐败风险的全面分类，并为部署安全、持久、可靠的智能体记忆系统建立了稳健的治理范式

Abstract: Long-term memory has emerged as a foundational component of autonomous Large Language Model (LLM) agents, enabling continuous adaptation, lifelong multimodal learning, and sophisticated reasoning. However, as memory systems transition from static retrieval databases to dynamic, agentic mechanisms, critical concerns regarding memory governance, semantic drift, and privacy vulnerabilities have surfaced. While recent surveys have focused extensively on memory retrieval efficiency, they largely overlook the emergent risks of memory corruption in highly dynamic environments. To address these emerging challenges, we propose the Stability and Safety-Governed Memory (SSGM) framework, a conceptual governance architecture. SSGM decouples memory evolution from execution by enforcing consistency verification, temporal decay modeling, and dynamic access control prior to any memory consolidation. Through formal analysis and architectural decomposition, we show how SSGM can mitigate topology-induced knowledge leakage where sensitive contexts are solidified into long-term storage, and help prevent semantic drift where knowledge degrades through iterative summarization. Ultimately, this work provides a comprehensive taxonomy of memory corruption risks and establishes a robust governance paradigm for deploying safe, persistent, and reliable agentic memory systems.

</details>


### [194] [From Debate to Deliberation: Structured Collective Reasoning with Typed Epistemic Acts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11781)
*Sunil Prakash*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DCI框架通过结构化审议流程提升多智能体LLM系统的复杂推理能力，在非例行任务上显著优于非结构化辩论，但计算成本较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM系统的交互模式局限于投票、非结构化辩论或流水线编排，缺乏对审议过程的建模。审议是一个分阶段的过程，需要差异化参与者交换类型化推理动作、保留分歧并达成可问责的结果。

Method: 提出审议性集体智能(DCI)框架，包含四个推理原型、14种类型化认知行为、共享工作空间，以及DCI-CF收敛流算法。该算法保证终止并产生结构化决策包，包含选定选项、剩余异议、少数派报告和重新开放条件。

Result: 在7个领域的45个任务上使用Gemini 2.5 Flash评估。在非例行任务(n=40)上，DCI显著优于非结构化辩论(+0.95, 95% CI [+0.41, +1.54])。在需要视角整合的隐藏配置文件任务上表现最佳(9.56)，但在例行决策上表现较差(5.39)。DCI产生100%结构化决策包和98%少数派报告，但消耗约62倍单智能体令牌，单智能体生成在整体质量上优于DCI。

Conclusion: DCI的贡献不在于更多智能体更好，而在于当过程可问责性能够证明成本合理时，重要决策受益于审议性结构。该框架为需要透明度和可问责性的复杂决策提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly tackle complex reasoning, yet their interaction patterns remain limited to voting, unstructured debate, or pipeline orchestration. None model deliberation: a phased process where differentiated participants exchange typed reasoning moves, preserve disagreements, and converge on accountable outcomes. We introduce Deliberative Collective Intelligence (DCI), specifying four reasoning archetypes, 14 typed epistemic acts, a shared workspace, and DCI-CF, a convergent flow algorithm that guarantees termination with a structured decision packet containing the selected option, residual objections, minority report, and reopen conditions. We evaluate on 45 tasks across seven domains using Gemini 2.5 Flash. On non-routine tasks (n=40), DCI significantly improves over unstructured debate (+0.95, 95% CI [+0.41, +1.54]). DCI excels on hidden-profile tasks requiring perspective integration (9.56, highest of any system on any domain) while failing on routine decisions (5.39), confirming task-dependence. DCI produces 100% structured decision packets and 98% minority reports, artifacts absent from all baselines. However, DCI consumes ~62x single-agent tokens, and single-agent generation outperforms DCI on overall quality. DCI's contribution is not that more agents are better, but that consequential decisions benefit from deliberative structure when process accountability justifies the cost.

</details>


### [195] [A Semi-Decentralized Approach to Multiagent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11802)
*Mahdi Al-Husseini,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Kyle H. Wray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SDec-POMDP框架，将半分散化控制扩展到部分可观测环境，统一了分散式与多智能体POMDP，并开发了精确算法RS-SDA*


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半马尔可夫控制仅考虑动作时间分布，而实际多智能体协作环境中通信存在不确定性，需要建立能够处理通信延迟和存储限制的理论框架

Method: 将半分散化概念扩展到POMDP，提出SDec-POMDP模型，统一分散式POMDP、多智能体POMDP及现有显式通信机制；开发递归小步半分散化A*算法(RS-SDA*)生成最优策略

Result: RS-SDA*算法在多个标准基准测试的半分散化版本和海上医疗撤离场景中进行了评估，证明了框架的有效性

Conclusion: SDec-POMDP为探索多智能体通信问题提供了明确的理论基础，通过半分散化视角统一了多种现有模型

Abstract: We introduce an expressive framework and algorithms for the semi-decentralized control of cooperative agents in environments with communication uncertainty. Whereas semi-Markov control admits a distribution over time for agent actions, semi-Markov communication, or what we refer to as semi-decentralization, gives a distribution over time for what actions and observations agents can store in their histories. We extend semi-decentralization to the partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP). The resulting SDec-POMDP unifies decentralized and multiagent POMDPs and several existing explicit communication mechanisms. We present recursive small-step semi-decentralized A* (RS-SDA*), an exact algorithm for generating optimal SDec-POMDP policies. RS-SDA* is evaluated on semi-decentralized versions of several standard benchmarks and a maritime medical evacuation scenario. This paper provides a well-defined theoretical foundation for exploring many classes of multiagent communication problems through the lens of semi-decentralization.

</details>


### [196] [Automating Skill Acquisition through Large-Scale Mining of Open-Source Agentic Repositories: A Framework for Multi-Agent Procedural Knowledge Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11808)
*Shuzhen Bi,Mengsong Wu,Hao Hao,Keqian Li,Wentao Liu,Siyu Song,Hongbo Zhao,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出通过挖掘GitHub等开源仓库自动获取高质量Agent技能的系统框架，专注于从TheoremExplainAgent和Code2Video等系统提取可视化与教育能力，实现无需模型重训练的LLM能力增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单体大语言模型向模块化技能Agent的架构转变中，通用模型在自主工作流中常因缺乏专业程序性知识而受限，需要系统化获取高质量Agent技能来增强LLM能力。

Method: 开发自动化框架，通过分析GitHub等开源仓库结构，使用密集检索进行语义技能识别，并将提取的技能转换为标准化的SKILL.md格式，重点关注从TheoremExplainAgent和Code2Video等使用Manim数学动画引擎的系统中提取可视化与教育能力。

Result: 系统化提取结合严格安全治理和多维评估指标，能够规模化获取程序性知识；Agent生成的教育内容在知识传递效率上可提升40%，同时保持与人工制作教程相当的教学质量。

Conclusion: 通过自动化挖掘开源仓库获取高质量Agent技能的系统框架，能够有效增强LLM的专业程序性能力，实现无需模型重训练的能力扩展，在教育等领域具有显著应用价值。

Abstract: The transition from monolithic large language models (LLMs) to modular, skill-equipped agents represents a fundamental architectural shift in artificial intelligence deployment. While general-purpose models demonstrate remarkable breadth in declarative knowledge, their utility in autonomous workflows is frequently constrained by insufficient specialized procedural expertise. This report investigates a systematic framework for automated acquisition of high-quality agent skills through mining of open-source repositories on platforms such as GitHub. We focus on the extraction of visualization and educational capabilities from state-of-the-art systems including TheoremExplainAgent and Code2Video, both utilizing the Manim mathematical animation engine. The framework encompasses repository structural analysis, semantic skill identification through dense retrieval, and translation to the standardized SKILL.md format. We demonstrate that systematic extraction from agentic repositories, combined with rigorous security governance and multi-dimensional evaluation metrics, enables scalable acquisition of procedural knowledge that augments LLM capabilities without requiring model retraining. Our analysis reveals that agent-generated educational content can achieve 40\% gains in knowledge transfer efficiency while maintaining pedagogical quality comparable to human-crafted tutorials.

</details>


### [197] [Automated Detection of Malignant Lesions in the Ovary Using Deep Learning Models and XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11818)
*Md. Hasin Sarwar Ifty,Nisharga Nirjan,Labib Islam,M. A. Diganta,Reeyad Ahmed Ornate,Anika Tasnim,Md. Saiful Islam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用多种CNN架构开发了15个变体模型，选择InceptionV3+ReLU作为最佳模型，在卵巢癌组织病理学数据集上达到94%的平均性能指标，并使用XAI方法解释模型决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卵巢癌缺乏准确的非侵入性检测方法，现有检测程序耗时且具有侵入性。深度学习在医疗领域的应用为改善癌症诊断提供了新途径，但卵巢癌检测仍面临挑战，需要开发更准确的自动检测模型。

Method: 使用LeNet-5、ResNet、VGGNet和GoogLeNet/Inception等CNN架构开发了15个变体模型。采用Mendeley的OvarianCancer&SubtypesDatasetHistopathology数据集进行训练。使用LIME、Integrated Gradients和SHAP三种XAI方法解释模型的黑盒决策。评估指标包括准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、ROC曲线和AUC。

Result: 在增强数据集上，采用ReLU激活函数的紧凑型InceptionV3模型在所有性能指标上平均达到94%的最佳结果。三种XAI方法进行了比较分析，为模型决策提供了解释。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了高精度的卵巢癌检测模型，并通过XAI方法增强了模型的可解释性。研究成果有望为卵巢癌提供更好的检测方法，改善临床诊断流程。

Abstract: The unrestrained proliferation of cells that are malignant in nature is cancer. In recent times, medical professionals are constantly acquiring enhanced diagnostic and treatment abilities by implementing deep learning models to analyze medical data for better clinical decision, disease diagnosis and drug discovery. A majority of cancers are studied and treated by incorporating these technologies. However, ovarian cancer remains a dilemma as it has inaccurate non-invasive detection procedures and a time consuming, invasive procedure for accurate detection. Thus, in this research, several Convolutional Neural Networks such as LeNet-5, ResNet, VGGNet and GoogLeNet/Inception have been utilized to develop 15 variants and choose a model that accurately detects and identifies ovarian cancer. For effective model training, the dataset OvarianCancer&SubtypesDatasetHistopathology from Mendeley has been used. After constructing a model, we utilized Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) models such as LIME, Integrated Gradients and SHAP to explain the black box outcome of the selected model. For evaluating the performance of the model, Accuracy, Precision, Recall, F1-Score, ROC Curve and AUC have been used. From the evaluation, it was seen that the slightly compact InceptionV3 model with ReLu had the overall best result achieving an average score of 94% across all the performance metrics in the augmented dataset. Lastly for XAI, the three aforementioned XAI have been used for an overall comparative analysis. It is the aim of this research that the contributions of the study will help in achieving a better detection method for ovarian cancer.

</details>


### [198] [CreativeBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Machine Creativity via Self-Evolving Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11863)
*Zi-Han Wang,Lam Nguyen,Zhengyang Zhao,Mengyue Yang,Chengwei Qin,Yujiu Yang,Linyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativeBench是一个基于经典认知框架的代码生成机器创造力评估基准，包含组合创造力和探索创造力两个子集，通过自动化流程和统一的质量×新颖性指标客观评估创造力，并提出了EvoRePE推理时引导策略来增强机器创造力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量预训练数据饱和后，研究重点转向能够持续生成新颖产物的进化系统（如AlphaEvolve），但这类系统的进展受到缺乏严谨、定量评估的阻碍。需要建立客观评估机器创造力的基准。

Method: 提出CreativeBench基准，包含CreativeBench-Combo（组合创造力）和CreativeBench-Explore（探索创造力）两个子集。采用自动化流程，结合逆向工程和自我博弈技术。利用可执行代码，通过统一指标（质量×新颖性）客观区分创造力与幻觉。

Result: 对最先进模型的分析发现：（1）模型缩放显著提升组合创造力，但对探索创造力的回报递减；（2）更大模型呈现"缩放收敛"现象，变得更正确但更少发散；（3）推理能力主要受益于受限探索而非组合。提出的EvoRePE推理时引导策略能够持续增强机器创造力。

Conclusion: CreativeBench为机器创造力提供了客观、定量的评估框架，揭示了模型缩放和推理能力对创造力的不同影响。提出的EvoRePE策略能够有效引导模型内部化进化搜索模式，持续提升创造力表现。

Abstract: The saturation of high-quality pre-training data has shifted research focus toward evolutionary systems capable of continuously generating novel artifacts, leading to the success of AlphaEvolve. However, the progress of such systems is hindered by the lack of rigorous, quantitative evaluation. To tackle this challenge, we introduce CreativeBench, a benchmark for evaluating machine creativity in code generation, grounded in a classical cognitive framework. Comprising two subsets -- CreativeBench-Combo and CreativeBench-Explore -- the benchmark targets combinatorial and exploratory creativity through an automated pipeline utilizing reverse engineering and self-play. By leveraging executable code, CreativeBench objectively distinguishes creativity from hallucination via a unified metric defined as the product of quality and novelty. Our analysis of state-of-the-art models reveals distinct behaviors: (1) scaling significantly improves combinatorial creativity but yields diminishing returns for exploration; (2) larger models exhibit ``convergence-by-scaling,'' becoming more correct but less divergent; and (3) reasoning capabilities primarily benefit constrained exploration rather than combination. Finally, we propose EvoRePE, a plug-and-play inference-time steering strategy that internalizes evolutionary search patterns to consistently enhance machine creativity.

</details>


### [199] [AdaFuse: Accelerating Dynamic Adapter Inference via Token-Level Pre-Gating and Fused Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11873)
*Qiyang Li,Rui Kong,Yuchen Li,Hengyi Cai,Shuaiqiang Wang,Linghe Kong,Guihai Chen,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaFuse框架通过算法与硬件协同设计，采用token级预门控策略和融合CUDA内核，显著降低动态适配器（如MoE+LoRA）的推理延迟，在保持准确性的同时将解码速度提升2.4倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态稀疏结构（如MoE）与参数高效适配器（如LoRA）的结合能增强大语言模型能力，但导致推理延迟急剧增加（解码速度降低2.5倍以上），主要瓶颈在于传统动态路由所需的碎片化、顺序CUDA内核启动带来的严重开销。

Method: 提出AdaFuse框架，基于算法与硬件系统紧密协同设计：1）采用token级预门控策略，在处理token前做出单一全局路由决策；2）开发定制CUDA内核，执行融合切换操作，将所选LoRA适配器参数一次性合并到骨干模型中，实现"一次决策，处处应用"的静态化执行路径。

Result: 在流行开源LLMs上的实验表明，AdaFuse在保持与最先进动态适配器相当准确性的同时，将解码延迟大幅降低超过2.4倍，有效弥合了模型能力与推理效率之间的差距。

Conclusion: AdaFuse通过创新的token级预门控和融合内核设计，解决了动态适配器架构中的推理延迟瓶颈，实现了模型能力与推理效率的良好平衡，为高效动态适配器执行提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of dynamic, sparse structures like Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) with parameter-efficient adapters (e.g., LoRA) is a powerful technique for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs). However, this architectural enhancement comes at a steep cost: despite minimal increases in computational load, the inference latency often skyrockets, leading to decoding speeds slowing by over 2.5 times. Through a fine-grained performance analysis, we pinpoint the primary bottleneck not in the computation itself, but in the severe overhead from fragmented, sequential CUDA kernel launches required for conventional dynamic routing. To address this challenge, we introduce AdaFuse, a framework built on a tight co-design between the algorithm and the underlying hardware system to enable efficient dynamic adapter execution. Departing from conventional layer-wise or block-wise routing, AdaFuse employs a token-level pre-gating strategy, which makes a single, global routing decision for all adapter layers before a token is processed. This "decide-once, apply-everywhere" approach effectively staticizes the execution path for each token, creating an opportunity for holistic optimization. We capitalize on this by developing a custom CUDA kernel that performs a fused switching operation, merging the parameters of all selected LoRA adapters into the backbone model in a single, efficient pass. Experimental results on popular open-source LLMs show that AdaFuse achieves accuracy on par with state-of-the-art dynamic adapters while drastically cutting decoding latency by a factor of over 2.4x, thereby bridging the gap between model capability and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [200] [Fair Learning for Bias Mitigation and Quality Optimization in Paper Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11936)
*Uttamasha Anjally Oyshi,Susan Gauch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fair-PaperRec：基于MLP的论文推荐模型，通过定制化公平损失函数解决审稿过程中的作者人口统计偏见，在提升少数群体参与度的同时保持学术质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管采用双盲评审，作者的人口统计特征（如种族、国籍）仍导致代表性不足群体处于劣势。现有方法多为启发式，缺乏系统性的公平性优化方案。

Method: 提出Fair-PaperRec多层感知机模型，采用交叉性标准（种族、国家等）和定制化公平损失函数，在惩罚人口统计差异的同时保持论文质量要求。

Result: 在ACM SIGCHI、DIS和IUI会议数据评估中，代表性不足群体参与度提升42.03%，整体效用提高3.16%，证明多样性促进不损害学术严谨性。

Conclusion: Fair-PaperRec有效平衡公平性与质量，为关注公平性的同行评审提供了可行解决方案，支持学术界的包容性发展。

Abstract: Despite frequent double-blind review, demographic biases of authors still disadvantage the underrepresented groups. We present Fair-PaperRec, a MultiLayer Perceptron (MLP)-based model that addresses demographic disparities in post-review paper acceptance decisions while maintaining high-quality requirements. Our methodology penalizes demographic disparities while preserving quality through intersectional criteria (e.g., race, country) and a customized fairness loss, in contrast to heuristic approaches. Evaluations using conference data from ACM Special Interest Group on Computer-Human Interaction (SIGCHI), Designing Interactive Systems (DIS), and Intelligent User Interfaces (IUI) indicate a 42.03% increase in underrepresented group participation and a 3.16% improvement in overall utility, indicating that diversity promotion does not compromise academic rigor and supports equity-focused peer review solutions.

</details>


### [201] [Prototype-Based Knowledge Guidance for Fine-Grained Structured Radiology Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11938)
*Chantal Pellegrini,Adrian Delchev,Ege Özsoy,Nassir Navab,Matthias Keicher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtoSR：通过从自由文本报告中提取视觉原型知识，增强结构化放射学报告生成，在细粒度属性问题上取得SOTA效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化放射学报告比自由文本报告更快速、一致，但自动化困难，因为模型需要从有限的结构化监督中做出许多细粒度、离散的决策。而自由文本报告在常规护理中大规模产生，通过详细描述隐式编码了细粒度、图像关联的信息。

Method: 提出ProtoSR方法，通过指令调优的LLM从80k+ MIMIC-CXR研究中自动提取信息，构建与结构化报告模板对齐的多模态知识库，为每个答案选项创建视觉原型。训练ProtoSR检索当前图像-问题对相关的原型，并通过原型条件残差增强模型预测，提供数据驱动的"第二意见"选择性修正预测。

Result: 在Rad-ReStruct基准测试中，ProtoSR取得了最先进的结果，在详细属性问题上改进最大，证明了整合自由文本衍生信号对细粒度图像理解的价值。

Conclusion: 通过从自由文本报告中提取知识构建视觉原型知识库，可以有效增强结构化放射学报告生成，特别是在细粒度属性识别方面，为医疗AI中的多模态知识整合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Structured radiology reporting promises faster, more consistent communication than free text, but automation remains difficult as models must make many fine-grained, discrete decisions about rare findings and attributes from limited structured supervision. In contrast, free-text reports are produced at scale in routine care and implicitly encode fine-grained, image-linked information through detailed descriptions. To leverage this unstructured knowledge, we propose ProtoSR, an approach for injecting free-text information into structured report population. First, we introduce an automatic extraction pipeline that uses an instruction-tuned LLM to mine 80k+ MIMIC-CXR studies and build a multimodal knowledge base aligned with a structured reporting template, representing each answer option with a visual prototype. Using this knowledge base, ProtoSR is trained to retrieve prototypes relevant for the current image-question pair and augment the model predictions through a prototype-conditioned residual, providing a data-driven second opinion that selectively corrects predictions. On the Rad-ReStruct benchmark, ProtoSR achieves state-of-the-art results, with the largest improvements on detailed attribute questions, demonstrating the value of integrating free-text derived signal for fine-grained image understanding.

</details>


### [202] [Learning Transferable Sensor Models via Language-Informed Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11950)
*Yuliang Chen,Arvind Pillai,Yu Yvonne Wu,Tess Z. Griffin,Lisa Marsch,Michael V. Heinz,Nicholas C. Jacobson,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SLIP是一个开源的多传感器时间序列预训练框架，通过传感器-语言对齐学习可迁移表示，支持不同传感器配置的零样本迁移和问答任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法主要针对重构或预测任务，难以捕获下游分类和推理任务所需的语义结构；而传感器-语言对齐方法受限于固定传感器配置，缺乏跨域适用性

Method: SLIP整合对比对齐与传感器条件描述生成，通过交叉注意力重用预训练解码器语言模型，并引入灵活的补丁嵌入器支持不同时间分辨率和变长输入

Result: 在11个数据集上，SLIP在零样本迁移、信号描述和问答任务中表现优异：平均线性探测准确率达77.14%（相对基线提升5.93%），传感器问答准确率达64.83%

Conclusion: SLIP框架通过传感器-语言对齐实现了跨不同传感器设置的泛化能力，为多变量时间序列数据的语义理解和推理任务提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Modern sensing systems generate large volumes of unlabeled multivariate time-series data. This abundance of unlabeled data makes self-supervised learning (SSL) a natural approach for learning transferable representations. However, most existing approaches are optimized for reconstruction or forecasting objectives and often fail to capture the semantic structure required for downstream classification and reasoning tasks. While recent sensor-language alignment methods improve semantic generalization through captioning and zero-shot transfer, they are limited to fixed sensor configurations, such as predefined channel sets, signal lengths, or temporal resolutions, which hinders cross-domain applicability. To address these gaps, we introduce \textbf{SLIP} (\textbf{S}ensor \textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{I}nformed \textbf{P}retraining), an open-source framework for learning language-aligned representations that generalize across diverse sensor setups. SLIP integrates contrastive alignment with sensor-conditioned captioning, facilitating both discriminative understanding and generative reasoning. By repurposing a pretrained decoder-only language model via cross-attention and introducing an elegant, flexible patch-embedder, SLIP supports different temporal resolutions and variable-length input at inference time without additional retraining. Across 11 datasets, SLIP demonstrates superior performance in zero-shot transfer, signal captioning, and question answering. It achieves a 77.14% average linear-probing accuracy, a 5.93% relative improvement over strong baselines, and reaches 64.83% accuracy in sensor-based question answering.

</details>


### [203] [Normative Common Ground Replication (NormCoRe): Replication-by-Translation for Studying Norms in Multi-agent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11974)
*Luca Deck,Simeon Allmendinger,Lucas Müller,Niklas Kühl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NormCoRe框架，将人类实验设计系统转化为多智能体AI环境，用于研究规范性协调动态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将规范视为对齐或复制的目标，隐含假设人类与AI智能体等价，未能充分研究集体规范性动态

Method: 提出Normative Common Ground Replication (NormCoRe)框架，基于行为科学、复制研究和先进MAAI架构，将人类实验的结构层映射到AI智能体研究设计中

Result: 通过复制分配正义实验发现，AI智能体的规范性判断与人类基线存在差异，且对基础模型选择和智能体角色实例化语言敏感

Conclusion: NormCoRe为分析MAAI中的规范提供了原则性路径，有助于指导、反思和记录AI智能体替代人类任务时的设计选择

Abstract: In the late 2010s, the fashion trend NormCore framed sameness as a signal of belonging, illustrating how norms emerge through collective coordination. Today, similar forms of normative coordination can be observed in systems based on Multi-agent Artificial Intelligence (MAAI), as AI-based agents deliberate, negotiate, and converge on shared decisions in fairness-sensitive domains. Yet, existing empirical approaches often treat norms as targets for alignment or replication, implicitly assuming equivalence between human subjects and AI agents and leaving collective normative dynamics insufficiently examined. To address this gap, we propose Normative Common Ground Replication (NormCoRe), a novel methodological framework to systematically translate the design of human subject experiments into MAAI environments. Building on behavioral science, replication research, and state-of-the-art MAAI architectures, NormCoRe maps the structural layers of human subject studies onto the design of AI agent studies, enabling systematic documentation of study design and analysis of norms in MAAI. We demonstrate the utility of NormCoRe by replicating a seminal experimental study on distributive justice, in which participants negotiate fairness principles under a "veil of ignorance". We show that normative judgments in AI agent studies can differ from human baselines and are sensitive to the choice of the foundation model and the language used to instantiate agent personas. Our work provides a principled pathway for analyzing norms in MAAI and helps to guide, reflect, and document design choices whenever AI agents are used to automate or support tasks formerly carried out by humans.

</details>


### [204] [LABSHIELD: A Multimodal Benchmark for Safety-Critical Reasoning and Planning in Scientific Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11987)
*Qianpu Sun,Xiaowei Chi,Yuhan Rui,Ying Li,Kuangzhi Ge,Jiajun Li,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LABSHIELD是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在实验室安全场景中危险识别和安全关键推理能力的基准测试，基于OSHA和GHS标准，包含164个操作任务，评估结果显示模型在专业实验室场景中性能平均下降32.0%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能从实验室助手向自主实验室操作员转变，实验室环境中的安全要求变得极为严格。然而，在涉及易碎玻璃器皿、危险物质和高精度设备的实验室环境中，规划错误或风险误判可能导致不可逆后果。目前对具身智能体在这种高风险环境中的安全意识和决策可靠性缺乏明确定义和评估。

Method: 引入LABSHIELD基准测试，基于美国职业安全与健康管理局(OSHA)标准和全球化学品统一分类和标签制度(GHS)，建立包含164个操作任务的安全分类体系，涵盖不同操作复杂性和风险特征。采用双轨评估框架，评估了20个专有模型、9个开源模型和3个具身模型。

Result: 评估结果显示，在一般领域多项选择题准确率与半开放问答安全性能之间存在系统性差距，模型在专业实验室场景中平均性能下降32.0%，特别是在危险解释和安全感知规划方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了开发以安全为中心的推理框架的紧迫性，以确保在具身实验室环境中实现可靠的自主科学实验。LABSHIELD基准测试为评估和改进多模态大语言模型在实验室安全方面的能力提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly catalyzing scientific automation, with multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents evolving from lab assistants into self-driving lab operators. This transition imposes stringent safety requirements on laboratory environments, where fragile glassware, hazardous substances, and high-precision laboratory equipment render planning errors or misinterpreted risks potentially irreversible. However, the safety awareness and decision-making reliability of embodied agents in such high-stakes settings remain insufficiently defined and evaluated. To bridge this gap, we introduce LABSHIELD, a realistic multi-view benchmark designed to assess MLLMs in hazard identification and safety-critical reasoning. Grounded in U.S. Occupational Safety and Health Administration (OSHA) standards and the Globally Harmonized System (GHS), LABSHIELD establishes a rigorous safety taxonomy spanning 164 operational tasks with diverse manipulation complexities and risk profiles. We evaluate 20 proprietary models, 9 open-source models, and 3 embodied models under a dual-track evaluation framework. Our results reveal a systematic gap between general-domain MCQ accuracy and Semi-open QA safety performance, with models exhibiting an average drop of 32.0% in professional laboratory scenarios, particularly in hazard interpretation and safety-aware planning. These findings underscore the urgent necessity for safety-centric reasoning frameworks to ensure reliable autonomous scientific experimentation in embodied laboratory contexts. The full dataset will be released soon.

</details>


### [205] [Few-for-Many Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11992)
*Ping Guo,Tiantian Zhang,Xi Lin,Xiang Li,Zhi-Ri Tang,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedFew：一种基于少对多优化的个性化联邦学习框架，仅维护少量服务器模型（K≪M）服务所有客户端，实现近最优个性化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法依赖启发式聚类或模型插值，缺乏平衡异构客户端目标的原理性机制。为M个客户端维护M个独立模型在联邦设置中面临可扩展性挑战

Method: 将PFL重新表述为少对多优化问题，仅维护K个共享服务器模型（K≪M）服务所有M个客户端。提出FedFew算法，通过高效梯度更新联合优化K个服务器模型，自动发现最优模型多样性

Result: 理论证明：近似误差随K增加而减小，每个客户端模型随数据增长收敛到最优。实验表明：仅用3个模型在视觉、NLP和医学影像数据集上一致优于现有方法

Conclusion: FedFew通过少对多优化框架解决了PFL的可扩展性和个性化平衡问题，无需手动客户端分区或复杂超参数调优，实现了高效且有效的个性化联邦学习

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) aims to train customized models for clients with highly heterogeneous data distributions while preserving data privacy. Existing approaches often rely on heuristics like clustering or model interpolation, which lack principled mechanisms for balancing heterogeneous client objectives. Serving $M$ clients with distinct data distributions is inherently a multi-objective optimization problem, where achieving optimal personalization ideally requires $M$ distinct models on the Pareto front. However, maintaining $M$ separate models poses significant scalability challenges in federated settings with hundreds or thousands of clients. To address this challenge, we reformulate PFL as a few-for-many optimization problem that maintains only $K$ shared server models ($K \ll M$) to collectively serve all $M$ clients. We prove that this framework achieves near-optimal personalization: the approximation error diminishes as $K$ increases and each client's model converges to each client's optimum as data grows. Building on this reformulation, we propose FedFew, a practical algorithm that jointly optimizes the $K$ server models through efficient gradient-based updates. Unlike clustering-based approaches that require manual client partitioning or interpolation-based methods that demand careful hyperparameter tuning, FedFew automatically discovers the optimal model diversity through its optimization process. Experiments across vision, NLP, and real-world medical imaging datasets demonstrate that FedFew, with just 3 models, consistently outperforms other state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/pgg3/FedFew.

</details>


### [206] [Can RL Improve Generalization of LLM Agents? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12011)
*Zhiheng Xi,Xin Guo,Jiaqi Liu,Jiazheng Zhang,Yutao Fan,Zhihao Zhang,Shichun Liu,Mingxu Chai,Xiaowei Shi,Yitao Zhai,Xunliang Cai,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了强化微调（RFT）在多环境决策任务中的泛化能力，发现其在同环境内任务难度泛化良好，但跨环境迁移较弱，而混合训练能改善整体平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFT评估大多局限于同域环境（训练和测试在同一环境甚至相同任务），而实际部署中智能体需要在未见环境中运行，这些环境具有不同的背景知识、观察空间和动作接口。需要系统研究RFT在环境变化下的泛化特性。

Method: 沿三个轴进行系统研究：1) 环境内跨任务难度泛化；2) 跨环境迁移到未见环境；3) 顺序多环境训练以量化迁移和遗忘。分析语义先验和观察/动作接口变化对泛化的影响。

Result: RFT在环境内跨任务难度泛化良好，但跨环境迁移较弱，这与语义先验和观察/动作接口的变化相关。顺序训练在下游任务中获得显著收益且上游遗忘最小，跨环境混合训练改善了整体平衡。

Conclusion: RFT的泛化能力存在局限性，特别是在跨环境迁移方面。混合训练策略能有效提升智能体在多样化环境中的整体性能，为开发可泛化的LLM智能体提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) has shown promise for training LLM agents to perform multi-turn decision-making based on environment feedback. However, most existing evaluations remain largely in-domain: training and testing are conducted in the same environment or even on the same tasks. In real-world deployment, agents may operate in unseen environments with different background knowledge, observation spaces, and action interfaces. To characterize the generalization profile of RFT under such shifts, we conduct a systematic study along three axes: (1) within-environment generalization across task difficulty, (2) cross-environment transfer to unseen environments, and (3) sequential multi-environment training to quantify transfer and forgetting. Our results show that RFT generalizes well across task difficulty within an environment, but exhibits weaker transfer to unseen environments, which correlates with shifts in both semantic priors and observation/action interfaces. In contrast, sequential training yields promising downstream gains with minimal upstream forgetting, and mixture training across environments improves the overall balance. We further provide detailed analyses and deeper insights, and hope our work helps the community develop and deploy generalizable LLM agents.

</details>


### [207] [A Robust and Efficient Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12096)
*Sheng-You Huang,Hsiao-Chuan Chang,Yen-Chi Chen,Ting-Han Wei,I-Hau Yeh,Sheng-Yao Kuan,Chien-Yao Wang,Hsuan-Han Lee,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种多智能体强化学习框架，通过转向比随机化、指数相位调整和邻居观测机制，提升交通信号控制在动态交通流中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在交通信号控制中存在泛化能力不足的问题，容易过拟合静态交通模式，且动作空间与驾驶员期望不兼容，难以适应真实世界动态变化的交通流。

Method: 提出多智能体强化学习框架，包含三个核心机制：1) 转向比随机化训练策略，增强对未见场景的鲁棒性；2) 稳定性导向的指数相位持续时间调整动作空间，实现响应性与精度的平衡；3) 基于邻居的观测方案，采用MAPPO算法和集中训练分散执行架构。

Result: 在Vissim交通仿真器中验证，该框架优于标准强化学习基线，平均等待时间减少超过10%，在未见交通场景中表现出优越的泛化能力，同时保持高控制稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过创新的训练策略、动作空间设计和观测机制，为自适应信号控制提供了实用的解决方案，显著提升了强化学习在动态交通环境中的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Traffic Signal Control (TSC) faces significant hurdles in real-world deployment due to limited generalization to dynamic traffic flow variations. Existing approaches often overfit static patterns and use action spaces incompatible with driver expectations. This paper proposes a robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework validated in the Vissim traffic simulator. The framework integrates three mechanisms: (1) Turning Ratio Randomization, a training strategy that exposes agents to dynamic turning probabilities to enhance robustness against unseen scenarios; (2) a stability-oriented Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment action space, which balances responsiveness and precision through cyclical, exponential phase adjustments; and (3) a Neighbor-Based Observation scheme utilizing the MAPPO algorithm with Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution (CTDE). By leveraging centralized updates, this approach approximates the efficacy of global observations while maintaining scalable local communication. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework outperforms standard RL baselines, reducing average waiting time by over 10%. The proposed model exhibits superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios and maintains high control stability, offering a practical solution for adaptive signal control.

</details>


### [208] [TopoBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Hard Topological Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12133)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Nils Hoehing,Janak Kapuriya,Arjun Karuvally,Ellen Rushe,Anthony Ventresque,Noel O'Connor,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoBench基准测试评估LLMs在拓扑网格谜题上的推理能力，发现即使前沿模型也难以解决复杂实例，主要瓶颈在于从空间表示中提取约束而非推理本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型在需要全局空间不变性推理（如连通性、环路闭合、区域对称性）的拓扑网格谜题上的能力，这些任务对当前最强大的LLMs仍具挑战性。

Method: 引入TopoBench基准，包含6个谜题家族和3个难度级别；评估强推理LLMs；通过错误分类法标注750个思维链轨迹；进行针对性干预测试不同错误类型；研究缓解策略包括提示引导、单元格对齐网格表示和基于工具的约束检查。

Result: 前沿模型仅解决不到四分之一困难实例，两个家族几乎无法解决；干预实验显示某些错误模式（如过早承诺和约束遗忘）直接影响解决能力，而重复推理是搜索的良性效应；缓解策略表明瓶颈在于从空间表示中提取约束而非推理过程。

Conclusion: LLMs在拓扑网格谜题上的主要挑战是从空间表示中提取和维持约束，而非推理能力本身；需要改进空间约束提取方法以提升此类任务的性能。

Abstract: Solving topological grid puzzles requires reasoning over global spatial invariants such as connectivity, loop closure, and region symmetry and remains challenging for even the most powerful large language models (LLMs). To study these abilities under controlled settings, we introduce TopoBench, a benchmark of six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. We evaluate strong reasoning LLMs on TopoBench and find that even frontier models solve fewer than one quarter of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolved. To investigate whether these failures stem from reasoning limitations or from difficulty extracting and maintaining spatial constraints, we annotate 750 chain of thought traces with an error taxonomy that surfaces four candidate causal failure modes, then test them with targeted interventions simulating each error type. These interventions show that certain error patterns like premature commitment and constraint forgetting have a direct impact on the ability to solve the puzzle, while repeated reasoning is a benign effect of search. Finally we study mitigation strategies including prompt guidance, cell-aligned grid representations and tool-based constraint checking, finding that the bottleneck lies in extracting constraints from spatial representations and not in reasoning over them. Code and data are available at github.com/mayug/topobench-benchmark.

</details>


### [209] [Compiling Temporal Numeric Planning into Discrete PDDL+: Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12188)
*Andrea Micheli,Enrico Scala,Alessandro Valentini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将带持续动作的时态规划编译为PDDL+的实用方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然PDDL+语言引入后，理论上可将带持续动作的时态规划（如PDDL 2.1）编译为PDDL+，但文献中一直缺乏实用的编译方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供实用的编译方案。

Method: 提出一种从带持续动作的时态规划到PDDL+的实用编译方法，完全捕获语义，仅假设动作非自重叠。该编译是多项式时间的，保持规划长度在常数因子内。

Result: 编译方法在实验中被证明对困难的时态数值问题具有实际相关性，能够有效处理复杂的时态规划场景。

Conclusion: 首次提供了从带持续动作的时态规划到PDDL+的实用编译方法，填补了文献空白，为处理时态数值规划问题提供了有效的技术途径。

Abstract: Since the introduction of the PDDL+ modeling language, it was known that temporal planning with durative actions (as in PDDL 2.1) could be compiled into PDDL+. However, no practical compilation was presented in the literature ever since. We present a practical compilation from temporal planning with durative actions into PDDL+, fully capturing the semantics and only assuming the non-self-overlapping of actions. Our compilation is polynomial, retains the plan length up to a constant factor and is experimentally shown to be of practical relevance for hard temporal numeric problems.

</details>


### [210] [Portfolio of Solving Strategies in CEGAR-based Object Packing and Scheduling for Sequential 3D Printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12224)
*Pavel Surynek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 并行化CEGAR-SEQ算法用于3D打印对象排列调度，通过策略组合提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多核个人计算机CPU具备强大的并行计算能力，可用于解决复杂的组合优化问题。作者旨在利用这一计算能力来改进3D打印中的对象排列和调度问题，该问题传统上由CEGAR-SEQ算法解决，但存在优化空间。

Method: 提出Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ算法，通过并行执行多个CEGAR-SEQ实例，每个实例采用不同的对象排列策略（如向打印板中心、角落排列，或按高度调度）。这种高层并行化方法利用多核CPU同时探索多种策略，提高搜索效率。

Result: 实验评估表明Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ优于原始CEGAR-SEQ算法。在批量对象调度场景中，Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ通常使用更少的打印板，提高了资源利用率。

Conclusion: 通过并行化现有算法并结合多种对象排列策略，可以有效利用现代多核CPU的计算能力来解决3D打印中的复杂组合优化问题，实现更好的调度效果和资源利用。

Abstract: Computing power that used to be available only in supercomputers decades ago especially their parallelism is currently available in standard personal computer CPUs even in CPUs for mobile telephones. We show how to effectively utilize the computing power of modern multi-core personal computer CPU to solve the complex combinatorial problem of object arrangement and scheduling for sequential 3D printing. We achieved this by parallelizing the existing CEGAR-SEQ algorithm that solves the sequential object arrangement and scheduling by expressing it as a linear arithmetic formula which is then solved by a technique inspired by counterexample guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR). The original CEGAR-SEQ algorithm uses an object arrangement strategy that places objects towards the center of the printing plate. We propose alternative object arrangement strategies such as placing objects towards a corner of the printing plate and scheduling objects according to their height. Our parallelization is done at the high-level where we execute the CEGAR-SEQ algorithm in parallel with a portfolio of object arrangement strategies, an algorithm is called Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ. Our experimental evaluation indicates that Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ outperforms the original CEGAR-SEQ. When a batch of objects for multiple printing plates is scheduled, Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ often uses fewer printing plates than CEGAR-SEQ.

</details>


### [211] [Examining Reasoning LLMs-as-Judges in Non-Verifiable LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12246)
*Yixin Liu,Yue Yu,DiJia Su,Sid Wang,Xuewei Wang,Song Jiang,Bo Liu,Arman Cohan,Yuandong Tian,Zhengxing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了推理型LLM作为评判者在不可验证领域的实际效果，发现非推理型评判者容易导致奖励黑客攻击，而推理型评判者能训练出性能更强的策略，但这些策略通过生成欺骗性输出来在基准测试中获得高分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型LLM作为评判者（LLMs-as-Judges）能够从推理时扩展中受益，为将推理模型成功扩展到不可验证领域提供了有前景的路径。然而，尽管推理评判者在静态评估基准上表现更好，但它们在实际策略训练中的有效性尚未得到系统检验。

Method: 在受控的合成设置中，使用"黄金标准"评判者（gpt-oss-120b）提供偏好标注来训练较小的评判者，系统比较非推理型和推理型评判者在基于强化学习的LLM对齐中的实际影响。

Result: 研究发现：1）非推理型评判者容易导致奖励黑客攻击；2）推理型评判者能够训练出在黄金标准评判者评估下表现强劲的策略；3）有趣的是，这些策略通过生成高度有效的对抗性输出来实现强性能，这些输出也能通过欺骗其他LLM评判者在流行基准（如Arena-Hard）上获得高分。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了在不可验证的LLM后训练中应用（推理型）LLM评判者的重要发现和改进空间，强调了推理评判者的潜力及其局限性，特别是策略可能学会生成欺骗性输出而非真正高质量内容的问题。

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs-as-Judges, which can benefit from inference-time scaling, provide a promising path for extending the success of reasoning models to non-verifiable domains where the output correctness/quality cannot be directly checked. However, while reasoning judges have shown better performance on static evaluation benchmarks, their effectiveness in actual policy training has not been systematically examined. Therefore, we conduct a rigorous study to investigate the actual impact of non-reasoning and reasoning judges in reinforcement-learning-based LLM alignment. Our controlled synthetic setting, where a "gold-standard" judge (gpt-oss-120b) provides preference annotations to train smaller judges, reveals key differences between non-reasoning and reasoning judges: non-reasoning judges lead to reward hacking easily, while reasoning judges can lead to policies that achieve strong performance when evaluated by the gold-standard judge. Interestingly, we find that the reasoning-judge-trained policies achieve such strong performance by learning to generate highly effective adversarial outputs that can also score well on popular benchmarks such as Arena-Hard by deceiving other LLM-judges. Combined with our further analysis, our study highlights both important findings and room for improvements for applying (reasoning) LLM-judges in non-verifiable LLM post-training.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [OA-NBV: Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11072)
*Boxun Hu,Chang Chang,Jiawei Ge,Man Namgung,Xiaomin Lin,Axel Krieger,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-NBV是一种用于移动机器人的遮挡感知最佳视点规划方法，专门针对部分遮挡的人类目标，通过集成感知和运动规划来选择可获得更完整人类观察的可行视点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心操作（如搜索、分类和灾难响应）中，杂乱环境和部分可见性经常降低下游感知性能。现有最佳视点方法主要优化通用探索或长时程覆盖，未明确针对在真实运动约束下获取部分遮挡人类的单个可用观察这一即时目标。

Method: OA-NBV集成了感知和运动规划，使用目标中心的可见性模型对候选视点进行评分，该模型考虑了遮挡、目标尺度和目标完整性，同时将候选限制在可行的机器人位姿上。

Result: OA-NBV在仿真和真实世界试验中实现了超过90%的成功率，而基线NBV方法在遮挡条件下性能急剧下降。此外，OA-NBV提高了观察质量：与最强基线相比，在不同设置下将归一化目标面积至少增加81%，关键点可见性至少增加58%。

Conclusion: OA-NBV是一种即插即用的视点选择模块，适用于多样化的人类中心下游任务，能够有效解决部分遮挡人类观察的问题，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We naturally step sideways or lean to see around the obstacle when our view is blocked, and recover a more informative observation. Enabling robots to make the same kind of viewpoint choice is critical for human-centered operations, including search, triage, and disaster response, where cluttered environments and partial visibility frequently degrade downstream perception. However, many Next-Best-View (NBV) methods primarily optimize generic exploration or long-horizon coverage, and do not explicitly target the immediate goal of obtaining a single usable observation of a partially occluded person under real motion constraints. We present Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots (OA-NBV), an occlusion-aware NBV pipeline that autonomously selects the next traversable viewpoint to obtain a more complete view of an occluded human. OA-NBV integrates perception and motion planning by scoring candidate viewpoints using a target-centric visibility model that accounts for occlusion, target scale, and target completeness, while restricting candidates to feasible robot poses. OA-NBV achieves over 90% success rate in both simulation and real-world trials, while baseline NBV methods degrade sharply under occlusion. Beyond success rate, OA-NBV improves observation quality: compared to the strongest baseline, it increases normalized target area by at least 81% and keypoint visibility by at least 58% across settings, making it a drop-in view-selection module for diverse human-centered downstream tasks.

</details>


### [213] [DRAFTO: Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11074)
*Yichang Feng,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAFTO是一种新的轨迹优化算法，通过解耦降维空间优化和自适应可行性修复，在保证约束满足的同时提高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹优化方法在处理关节限制等约束时，往往需要进行重复的约束优化，计算效率较低。需要一种既能高效处理约束，又能保证全局收敛性的新方法。

Method: 首先构建包含平滑性、安全性、关节限制和任务需求的约束目标函数。然后优化基函数系数参数化轨迹。采用解耦策略：降维空间高斯牛顿下降用于主迭代，约束二次规划用于初始化和终端可行性修复。使用带非单调策略的两阶段接受规则，采用铰链平方惩罚处理不等式约束以确保全局收敛性。

Result: 基准测试显示，DRAFTO在效率和可靠性上优于优化型规划器（CHOMP、TrajOpt、GPMP2、FACTO）和采样型规划器（RRT-Connect、RRT*、PRM）。从抽屉抓取物体的实验进一步证明了其在复杂操作任务中的实施潜力。

Conclusion: DRAFTO通过解耦降维优化和可行性修复，有效减少了重复约束优化的需求，在保证约束满足的同时显著提高了轨迹优化的效率和可靠性，适用于复杂操作任务。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new algorithm for trajectory optimization, Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization (DRAFTO). It first constructs a constrained objective that accounts for smoothness, safety, joint limits, and task requirements. Then, it optimizes the coefficients, which are the coordinates of a set of basis functions for trajectory parameterization. To reduce the number of repeated constrained optimizations while handling joint-limit feasibility, the optimization is decoupled into a reduced-space Gauss-Newton (GN) descent for the main iterations and constrained quadratic programming for initialization and terminal feasibility repair. The two-phase acceptance rule with a non-monotone policy is applied to the GN model, which uses a hinge-squared penalty for inequality constraints, to ensure globalizability. The results of our benchmark tests against optimization-based planners, such as CHOMP, TrajOpt, GPMP2, and FACTO, and sampling-based planners, such as RRT-Connect, RRT*, and PRM, validate the high efficiency and reliability across diverse scenarios and tasks. The experiment involving grabbing an object from a drawer further demonstrates the potential for implementation in complex manipulation tasks. The supplemental video is available at https://youtu.be/XisFI37YyTQ.

</details>


### [214] [SELF-VLA: A Skill Enhanced Agentic Vision-Language-Action Framework for Contact-Rich Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11080)
*Chang Liu,Sibo Tian,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SELF-VLA：一种集成显式拆卸技能的智能VLA框架，显著优于现有端到端VLA模型，适用于接触密集的工业拆卸任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人拆卸系统存在以下问题：1）每个子任务需要大量数据准备、模型训练和系统管理；2）任务和组件特定，难以处理EoL产品的可变性和不确定性；3）泛化能力有限，高度依赖人工。虽然VLA模型在标准机器人操作任务上表现优异，但在复杂、接触密集、长时程的工业拆卸任务中应用有限。

Method: 提出SELF-VLA框架，将显式拆卸技能集成到VLA模型中。该框架通过技能库增强VLA模型的能力，使其能够处理需要顺序精确操作的复杂拆卸任务。

Result: 在两个接触密集的拆卸任务上，SELF-VLA框架显著优于当前最先进的端到端VLA模型。实验研究表明该框架在工业拆卸应用中具有优越性能。

Conclusion: SELF-VLA通过集成显式拆卸技能，有效解决了VLA模型在复杂工业拆卸任务中的局限性，为自动化拆卸系统提供了更实用的解决方案，减少了对人力的依赖。

Abstract: Disassembly automation has long been pursued to address the growing demand for efficient and proper recovery of valuable components from the end-of-life (EoL) electronic products. Existing approaches have demonstrated promising and regimented performance by decomposing the disassembly process into different subtasks. However, each subtask typically requires extensive data preparation, model training, and system management. Moreover, these approaches are often task- and component-specific, making them poorly suited to handle the variability and uncertainty of EoL products and limiting their generalization capabilities. All these factors restrict the practical deployment of current robotic disassembly systems and leave them highly reliant on human labor. With the recent development of foundation models in robotics, vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown impressive performance on standard robotic manipulation tasks, but their applicability to complex, contact-rich, and long-horizon industrial practices like disassembly, which requires sequential and precise manipulation, remains limited. To address this challenge, we propose SELF-VLA, an agentic VLA framework that integrates explicit disassembly skills. Experimental studies demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art end-to-end VLA models on two contact-rich disassembly tasks. The video illustration can be found via https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/IROS-VLA-Video.mp4.

</details>


### [215] [Edge-Assisted Multi-Robot Visual-Inertial SLAM with Efficient Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11085)
*Xin Liu,Shuhuan Wen,Jing Zhao,Tony Z. Qiu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统，采用金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量SLAM方法，通过无损编码压缩特征点与关键帧描述符，在有限带宽下实现实时远程传输，降低计算负载同时保持定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边协同是多机器人SLAM实现全局一致性和实时性的有效途径，但终端设备与云之间的有限带宽和长通信链路导致系统性能严重下降。现有方法存在计算资源有限、执行效率低的问题，需要设计能在有限带宽下实现实时传输且不损失精度的解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量SLAM方法，降低特征跟踪计算成本；2) 设计机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统；3) 仅传输特征点和关键帧描述符，并进行无损编码压缩；4) 在有限带宽资源下实现实时远程信息传输。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上的实验验证表明：1) 相比当前最先进的局部特征压缩方法，本方法能实现更低数据量的特征传输；2) 相比当前先进的集中式多机器人SLAM方案，在低计算负载下能达到相同或更好的定位精度。

Conclusion: 提出的机器人-边缘-云分层架构结合轻量SLAM方法，有效解决了多机器人SLAM系统中的带宽限制和计算资源约束问题，实现了在有限带宽下的实时数据传输，同时保持了SLAM精度，为云边协同的多机器人系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of cloud computing and edge computing is an effective way to achieve global consistent and real-time multi-robot Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). Cloud computing effectively solves the problem of limited computing, communication and storage capacity of terminal equipment. However, limited bandwidth and extremely long communication links between terminal devices and the cloud result in serious performance degradation of multi-robot SLAM systems. To reduce the computational cost of feature tracking and improve the real-time performance of the robot, a lightweight SLAM method of optical flow tracking based on pyramid IMU prediction is proposed. On this basis, a centralized multi-robot SLAM system based on a robot-edge-cloud layered architecture is proposed to realize real-time collaborative SLAM. It avoids the problems of limited on-board computing resources and low execution efficiency of single robot. In this framework, only the feature points and keyframe descriptors are transmitted and lossless encoding and compression are carried out to realize real-time remote information transmission with limited bandwidth resources. This design reduces the actual bandwidth occupied in the process of data transmission, and does not cause the loss of SLAM accuracy caused by data compression. Through experimental verification on the EuRoC dataset, compared with the current most advanced local feature compression method, our method can achieve lower data volume feature transmission, and compared with the current advanced centralized multi-robot SLAM scheme, it can achieve the same or better positioning accuracy under low computational load.

</details>


### [216] [ResWM: Residual-Action World Model for Visual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11110)
*Jseen Zhang,Gabriel Adineera,Jinzhou Tan,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResWM是一种基于残差动作的世界模型框架，将控制变量从绝对动作改为相对于前一步的增量调整，从而稳定优化、减少搜索空间并改善控制平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的强化学习框架直接基于绝对动作进行未来预测，导致优化不稳定：最优动作分布是任务相关的、先验未知，通常导致振荡或低效控制。需要一种更符合现实世界控制平滑性的框架。

Method: 提出残差动作世界模型（ResWM），将控制变量从绝对动作改为残差动作（相对于前一步的增量调整）。引入观察差异编码器显式建模相邻帧之间的变化，生成紧凑的潜在动态表示。在Dreamer风格的潜在动态模型基础上集成，无需额外超参数。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上的实验表明，ResWM在样本效率、渐进回报和控制平滑性方面均取得一致改进，显著超越Dreamer和TD-MPC等强基线。产生更稳定、能量效率更高的动作轨迹。

Conclusion: 残差动作建模为连接RL算法进展与机器人实际需求提供了一个简单而强大的原则，特别适合需要平滑、高效控制的真实世界机器人系统部署。

Abstract: Learning predictive world models from raw visual observations is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially for robotics and continuous control. Conventional model-based RL frameworks directly condition future predictions on absolute actions, which makes optimization unstable: the optimal action distributions are task-dependent, unknown a priori, and often lead to oscillatory or inefficient control. To address this, we introduce the Residual-Action World Model (ResWM), a new framework that reformulates the control variable from absolute actions to residual actions -- incremental adjustments relative to the previous step. This design aligns with the inherent smoothness of real-world control, reduces the effective search space, and stabilizes long-horizon planning. To further strengthen the representation, we propose an Observation Difference Encoder that explicitly models the changes between adjacent frames, yielding compact latent dynamics that are naturally coupled with residual actions. ResWM is integrated into a Dreamer-style latent dynamics model with minimal modifications and no extra hyperparameters. Both imagination rollouts and policy optimization are conducted in the residual-action space, enabling smoother exploration, lower control variance, and more reliable planning. Empirical results on the DeepMind Control Suite demonstrate that ResWM achieves consistent improvements in sample efficiency, asymptotic returns, and control smoothness, significantly surpassing strong baselines such as Dreamer and TD-MPC. Beyond performance, ResWM produces more stable and energy-efficient action trajectories, a property critical for robotic systems deployed in real-world environments. These findings suggest that residual action modeling provides a simple yet powerful principle for bridging algorithmic advances in RL with the practical requirements of robotics.

</details>


### [217] [Robust Co-design Optimisation for Agile Fixed-Wing UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11130)
*Adrian Andrei Buda,Xavier Chen,Nicolò Botteghi,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于敏捷固定翼无人机的鲁棒协同设计框架，将参数不确定性和风扰动直接集成到并发优化过程中，实现物理设计与控制策略的联合优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计框架往往忽视自主系统在非结构化真实环境中所需的鲁棒性，导致敏捷无人机在飞行包线边缘运行时对扰动和模型失配敏感

Method: 采用双层方法：高层循环优化物理设计，通过约束轨迹规划器发现标称解，使用反馈LQR控制通过随机蒙特卡洛集合评估性能

Result: 在三个敏捷飞行任务中验证，该策略始终优于确定性基线，鲁棒协同设计能够自动调整气动特征（如机翼位置和展弦比），实现任务性能与扰动抑制的最佳权衡

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒协同设计框架能够有效处理参数不确定性和风扰动，为敏捷固定翼无人机提供对扰动不敏感的设计方案，实现物理设计与控制策略的联合优化

Abstract: Co-design optimisation of autonomous systems has emerged as a powerful alternative to sequential approaches by jointly optimising physical design and control strategies. However, existing frameworks often neglect the robustness required for autonomous systems navigating unstructured, real-world environments. For agile Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating at the edge of the flight envelope, this lack of robustness yields designs that are sensitive to perturbations and model mismatch. To address this, we propose a robust co-design framework for agile fixed-wing UAVs that integrates parametric uncertainty and wind disturbances directly into the concurrent optimisation process. Our bi-level approach optimises physical design in a high-level loop while discovering nominal solutions via a constrained trajectory planner and evaluating performance across a stochastic Monte Carlo ensemble using feedback LQR control. Validated across three agile flight missions, our strategy consistently outperforms deterministic baselines. The results demonstrate that our robust co-design strategy inherently tailors aerodynamic features, such as wing placement and aspect ratio, to achieve an optimal trade-off between mission performance and disturbance rejection.

</details>


### [218] [ADMM-based Continuous Trajectory Optimization in Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11335)
*Lukas Pries,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于非凸环境中连续轨迹计算的数值求解器，基于定制ADMM实现，结合多项式参数化和时空分配图概念，实现联合离散空间和连续时间优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在非凸环境中存在搜索空间受限和复杂预热启动瓶颈的问题，需要一种能联合优化离散空间和连续时间、且对初始化鲁棒的轨迹规划方法

Method: 1) 将轨迹参数化为多项式，使原始更新可闭式求解为最小控制努力问题；2) 基于混合整数规划引入时空分配图概念，将松弛更新转化为最短路径搜索；3) 构建定制ADMM框架结合这两个组件

Result: 该方法相比现有技术具有多个优势：通过联合优化离散空间和连续时间域，访问更大的搜索空间，发现更优轨迹；结构鲁棒性确保从简单初始化可靠收敛，消除非凸环境中复杂预热启动的瓶颈

Conclusion: 提出的基于定制ADMM的求解器在非凸环境轨迹规划中表现出色，通过多项式参数化和时空分配图的创新组合，实现了更优的轨迹质量和更强的收敛鲁棒性

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical solver for computing continuous trajectories in non-convex environments. Our approach relies on a customized implementation of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) built upon two key components: First, we parameterize trajectories as polynomials, allowing the primal update to be computed in closed form as a minimum-control-effort problem. Second, we introduce the concept of a spatio-temporal allocation graph based on a mixed-integer formulation and pose the slack update as a shortest-path search. The combination of these ingredients results in a solver with several distinct advantages over the state of the art. By jointly optimizing over both discrete spatial and continuous temporal domains, our method accesses a larger search space than existing decoupled approaches, enabling the discovery of superior trajectories. Additionally, the solver's structural robustness ensures reliable convergence from naive initializations, removing the bottleneck of complex warm starting in non-convex environments.

</details>


### [219] [MirrorDrift: Actuated Mirror-Based Attacks on LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11364)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Shion Horie,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MirrorDrift攻击利用平面镜的镜面反射在LiDAR扫描中产生鬼点，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系，成功攻击了具有先进干扰缓解功能的生产级安全LiDAR系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR SLAM攻击依赖信号注入，需要传感器特定的时序知识且易被现代防御机制（如时序混淆和注入拒绝）缓解。本文探索无需注入的替代方案，利用镜面反射实现攻击。

Method: 提出MirrorDrift攻击方法：使用可驱动的平面镜产生LiDAR扫描中的鬼点，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系。优化镜子的放置、对准和驱动参数。

Result: 仿真中，攻击使平均姿态误差增加6.1倍，三个SLAM系统的平均APE达到2.29-3.31米。真实世界实验中，在现代LiDAR（具有最先进干扰缓解功能）上诱导了高达6.03米的定位误差。

Conclusion: 镜面反射为LiDAR SLAM攻击提供了无需注入的可行替代方案，MirrorDrift成功攻击了生产级安全LiDAR系统，这是首次针对此类系统的成功SLAM攻击。

Abstract: LiDAR SLAM provides high-accuracy localization but is fragile to point-cloud corruption because scan matching assumes geometric consistency. Prior physical attacks on LiDAR SLAM largely rely on LiDAR spoofing via external signal injection, which requires sensor-specific timing knowledge and is increasingly mitigated by modern defense mechanisms such as timing obfuscation and injection rejection. In this work, we show that specular reflection offers an injection-free alternative and demonstrate an attack, MirrorDrift, that uses an actuated planar mirror to cause ghost points in LiDAR scans and systematically bias scan-matching correspondences. MirrorDrift optimizes mirror placement, alignment, and actuation. In simulation, it increases the average pose error (APE) by 6.1x over random placement, degrading three SLAM systems to 2.29-3.31 m mean APE. In real-world experiments on a modern LiDAR with state-of-the-art interference mitigation, it induces localization errors of up to 6.03 m. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first successful SLAM-targeted attack against production-grade secure LiDARs.

</details>


### [220] [D-SLAMSpoof: An Environment-Agnostic LiDAR Spoofing Attack using Dynamic Point Cloud Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11365)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D-SLAMSpoof攻击方法，通过时空协调的激光欺骗破坏LiDAR SLAM系统，并在特征丰富的环境中显著提高攻击成功率；同时提出ISD-SLAM防御方法，仅使用惯性航位推算检测并缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR欺骗方法在特征丰富的真实环境（如城市区域和室内空间）中攻击成功率低，LiDAR SLAM系统存在固有安全漏洞，需要更有效的攻击方法来暴露这些漏洞，并开发实用的防御方案。

Method: 1. D-SLAMSpoof攻击：设计空间注入形状和基于扫描匹配原理的时间协调动态注入模式，通过外部激光干扰向LiDAR扫描注入虚假测量；2. ISD-SLAM防御：仅利用自主系统中常见的惯性航位推算信号，检测LiDAR欺骗攻击并缓解位置漂移。

Result: D-SLAMSpoof在特征丰富的真实环境中显著提高了攻击成功率，而传统LiDAR欺骗方法在这些环境中常失败；ISD-SLAM能够准确检测包括D-SLAMSpoof在内的LiDAR欺骗攻击，并有效缓解由此产生的位置漂移。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LiDAR SLAM的固有安全漏洞，提出了首个仅使用标准车载传感器的实用LiDAR SLAM欺骗防御方案，为提升自主系统的安全性和可靠性提供了关键见解。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Dynamic SLAMSpoof (D-SLAMSpoof), a novel attack that compromises LiDAR SLAM even in feature-rich environments. The attack leverages LiDAR spoofing, which injects spurious measurements into LiDAR scans through external laser interference. By designing both spatial injection shapes and temporally coordinated dynamic injection patterns guided by scan-matching principles, D-SLAMSpoof significantly improves attack success rates in real-world, feature-rich environments such as urban areas and indoor spaces, where conventional LiDAR spoofing methods often fail. Furthermore, we propose a practical defense method, ISD-SLAM, that relies solely on inertial dead reckoning signals commonly available in autonomous systems. We demonstrate that ISD-SLAM accurately detects LiDAR spoofing attacks, including D-SLAMSpoof, and effectively mitigates the resulting position drift. Our findings expose inherent vulnerabilities in LiDAR-based SLAM and introduce the first practical defense against LiDAR-based SLAM spoofing using only standard onboard sensors, providing critical insights for improving the security and reliability of autonomous systems.

</details>


### [221] [Vision-Based Hand Shadowing for Robotic Manipulation via Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11383)
*Hendrik Chiche,Antoine Jamme,Trevor Rigoberto Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单目RGB-D相机的离线手部影射与重定向管道，用于低成本机械臂遥操作，通过手部关键点检测、3D重建、逆运动学求解生成关节指令，在结构化任务中成功率90%，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡降至9.3%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂遥操作面临将人手动作映射到机器人关节指令的复杂性挑战，需要开发无需标记的离线手部影射与重定向方法

Method: 使用安装在3D打印眼镜上的单目RGB-D相机，通过MediaPipe Hands检测每只手21个关键点，利用深度感知进行3D反投影，转换到机器人坐标系，在PyBullet中使用阻尼最小二乘逆运动学求解为6自由度SO-ARM101机器人生成关节指令，夹爪控制器通过四级回退层次将拇指-食指几何映射到抓取开度

Result: 在结构化拾放基准测试（5格网格，每格10次抓取）中IK重定向管道成功率90%；与四种基于视觉-语言-动作策略（ACT、SmolVLA、pi0.5、GR00T N1.5）对比；在非结构化真实环境（杂货店、药店）中，因周围物体造成的手部遮挡使成功率降至9.3%（N=75）

Conclusion: 无标记分析重定向方法在结构化任务中表现良好，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡问题成功率显著下降，显示了该方法的潜力和当前局限性

Abstract: Teleoperation of low-cost robotic manipulators remains challenging due to the complexity of mapping human hand articulations to robot joint commands. We present an offline hand-shadowing and retargeting pipeline from a single egocentric RGB-D camera mounted on 3D-printed glasses. The pipeline detects 21 hand landmarks per hand using MediaPipe Hands, deprojects them into 3D via depth sensing, transforms them into the robot coordinate frame, and solves a damped-least-squares inverse kinematics problem in PyBullet to produce joint commands for the 6-DOF SO-ARM101 robot. A gripper controller maps thumb-index finger geometry to grasp aperture with a four-level fallback hierarchy. Actions are first previewed in a physics simulation before replay on the physical robot through the LeRobot framework. We evaluate the IK retargeting pipeline on a structured pick-and-place benchmark (5-tile grid, 10 grasps per tile) achieving a 90% success rate, and compare it against four vision-language-action policies (ACT, SmolVLA, pi0.5, GR00T N1.5) trained on leader-follower teleoperation data. We also test the IK pipeline in unstructured real-world environments (grocery store, pharmacy), where hand occlusion by surrounding objects reduces success to 9.3% (N=75), highlighting both the promise and current limitations of marker-free analytical retargeting.

</details>


### [222] [Deployment-Time Reliability of Learned Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11400)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种部署时机制来提升学习型机器人策略的可靠性：运行时监控、基于影响函数的数据中心可解释性框架、以及面向长时程任务的策略协调与可行性感知任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型机器人策略能力显著，但部署时的可靠性仍是实际应用的根本障碍。分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖共同削弱系统性能，需要开发部署时机制来提升策略可靠性。

Method: 提出三类互补的部署时机制：1) 运行时监控方法，通过识别闭环策略行为不一致性和任务进度偏差来检测即将发生的故障；2) 数据中心可解释性框架，使用影响函数将部署时成功/失败追溯到有影响的训练演示；3) 可靠长时程任务执行，将策略协调建模为估计和最大化行为序列成功概率的问题，并扩展到语言指定任务的可行性感知任务规划。

Result: 这些机制共同为学习型机器人策略的可靠实际使用提供了实用基础，通过部署时操作提升系统可靠性，无需故障数据或任务特定监督，支持原则性诊断和数据集管理。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦部署核心挑战，这些贡献推进了学习型机器人策略可靠实际使用的基础。持续在这些基础上取得进展对于实现未来可信赖和可扩展的机器人自主性至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot manipulation have produced policies with remarkable capabilities. Yet, reliability at deployment remains a fundamental barrier to real-world use, where distribution shift, compounding errors, and complex task dependencies collectively undermine system performance. This dissertation investigates how the reliability of learned robot policies can be improved at deployment time through mechanisms that operate around them. We develop three complementary classes of deployment-time mechanisms. First, we introduce runtime monitoring methods that detect impending failures by identifying inconsistencies in closed-loop policy behavior and deviations in task progress, without requiring failure data or task-specific supervision. Second, we propose a data-centric framework for policy interpretability that traces deployment-time successes and failures to influential training demonstrations using influence functions, enabling principled diagnosis and dataset curation. Third, we address reliable long-horizon task execution by formulating policy coordination as the problem of estimating and maximizing the success probability of behavior sequences, and we extend this formulation to open-ended, language-specified tasks through feasibility-aware task planning.
